[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]

[Step 3/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product\n\n  // 1. unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  // 2. classify\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // 6. exponent of a normal result\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  // 8. pack\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

Now I understand the design. Let me analyze: the critical path is ~1338ps (deep path through the 22-iteration LZ loop, two shifters, and duplicated rounding). Let me create a much more efficient unified-shift implementation.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, unified single-shift architecture.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (\n  input  [15:0] a,\n  input  [15:0] b,\n  output [15:0] y\n);\n  wire        s_a = a[15], s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10], e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0],  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_az = ~|e_a, e_bz = ~|e_b;\n  wire e_am = &e_a,  e_bm = &e_b;\n  wire f_az = ~|f_a, f_bz = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = e_az & f_az;\n  wire zero_b = e_bz & f_bz;\n  wire inf_a  = e_am & f_az;\n  wire inf_b  = e_bm & f_bz;\n  wire nan_a  = e_am & ~f_az;\n  wire nan_b  = e_bm & ~f_bz;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_az, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_bz, f_b};\n  wire [21:0] P   = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // effective exponent sum (subnormal / zero uses 1)\n  wire [5:0] esum = (e_az ? 6'd1 : {1'b0,e_a}) + (e_bz ? 6'd1 : {1'b0,e_b});\n\n  // ---- leading-one position of P (0 if P==0) : 5-level binary search ----\n  reg  [4:0]  ppos;\n  reg  [21:0] tt;\n  always @* begin\n    if (|P[21:11]) begin tt = P >> 11; ppos = 5'd11; end\n    else           begin tt = P;        ppos = 5'd0;  end\n    if (|tt[10:6]) begin tt = tt >> 5; ppos = ppos + 5'd5; end\n    if (|tt[5:3])  begin tt = tt >> 3; ppos = ppos + 5'd3; end\n    if (tt[2])     begin tt = tt >> 2; ppos = ppos + 5'd2; end\n    if (tt[1])     ppos = ppos + 5'd1;\n  end\n\n  // ---- unified right shift amount d = max(p-10, 26-esum) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] A = {2'b0, ppos} - 7'sd10;\n  wire signed [6:0] B = 7'sd26 - {1'b0, esum};\n  wire        [4:0] d = (A > B) ? A[4:0] : B[4:0];\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter on P2 = {P,0} : R[k] = P[k+d-1], sticky = |P[d-2:0] ----\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {P, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? (P2 >> 1)  : P2;\n  wire        k1 = d[0] & P2[0];\n  wire [22:0] q2 = d[1] ? (q1 >> 2)  : q1;\n  wire        k2 = d[1] & (|q1[1:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q4 = d[2] ? (q2 >> 4)  : q2;\n  wire        k4 = d[2] & (|q2[3:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q8 = d[3] ? (q4 >> 8)  : q4;\n  wire        k8 = d[3] & (|q4[7:0]);\n  wire [22:0] R  = d[4] ? (q8 >> 16) : q8;\n  wire        k16 = d[4] & (|q8[15:0]);\n  wire        S  = k1 | k2 | k4 | k8 | k16;\n\n  wire [10:0] mant = R[11:1];\n  wire        G    = R[0];\n  wire        rup  = G & (S | mant[0]);\n  wire [11:0] rounded = {1'b0, mant} + {11'd0, rup};\n  wire [9:0]  frac = rounded[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire signed [6:0] E = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b0, ppos} - 7'd50;\n  wire is_sub = E < -14;\n  wire [6:0] yexp_n = E + 7'd15 + {6'd0, rounded[11]};\n  wire ovf = ~is_sub & (yexp_n >= 7'd31);\n  wire [4:0] e_res = is_sub ? {4'd0, rounded[10]} : (ovf ? 5'd31 : yexp_n[4:0]);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = inf_in | ovf;\n  wire [4:0] ey = is_nan ? 5'd31 : is_inf ? 5'd31 : zero_in ? 5'd0 : e_res;\n  wire [9:0] fy = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | zero_in) ? 10'd0 : frac;\n  wire       sy = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sy, ey, fy};\nendmodule\n", filename='design2.sv')

Now let me write a corrected, streamlined unified-shift version. I've carefully verified the math (deriving exponent/shift equations and checking against the 3 golden vectors).


  -> create_file(content='// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, unified single-shift architecture.\n//\n// Core idea: P = m_a*m_b (22 bits).  Let p = position of P\'s leading one.\n// Shifting P right by d = max(p-10, 26-esum) puts the significand on the\n// normal grid (leading one at bit 10) or, when 26-esum dominates, directly on\n// the subnormal grid.  The dominant term is exactly the "is subnormal result"\n// predicate, so both cases share one shifter, one rounder and one pack stage.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (\n  input  [15:0] a,\n  input  [15:0] b,\n  output [15:0] y\n);\n  wire        s_a = a[15], s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10], e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0],  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_az = ~|e_a, e_bz = ~|e_b;\n  wire e_am = &e_a,  e_bm = &e_b;\n  wire f_az = ~|f_a, f_bz = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = e_az & f_az;\n  wire zero_b = e_bz & f_bz;\n  wire inf_a  = e_am & f_az;\n  wire inf_b  = e_bm & f_bz;\n  wire nan_a  = e_am & ~f_az;\n  wire nan_b  = e_bm & ~f_bz;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_az, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_bz, f_b};\n  wire [21:0] P   = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // effective exponent sum (subnormal / zero uses 1)\n  wire [5:0] esum = (e_az ? 6\'d1 : {1\'b0, e_a}) + (e_bz ? 6\'d1 : {1\'b0, e_b});\n\n  // ---- leading-one position of P (0 if P==0): 5-level binary search ----\n  reg  [4:0]  ppos;\n  reg  [21:0] tt;\n  always @* begin\n    if (|P[21:11]) begin tt = P >> 11; ppos = 5\'d11; end\n    else           begin tt = P;        ppos = 5\'d0;  end\n    if (|tt[10:6]) begin tt = tt >> 5; ppos = ppos + 5\'d5; end\n    if (|tt[5:3])  begin tt = tt >> 3; ppos = ppos + 5\'d3; end\n    if (tt[2])     begin tt = tt >> 2; ppos = ppos + 5\'d2; end\n    if (tt[1])     ppos = ppos + 5\'d1;\n  end\n\n  // ---- unified right shift d = max(p-10, 26-esum) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] sh_n = {2\'b00, ppos} - 7\'sd10;\n  wire signed [6:0] sh_s = 7\'sd26 - {1\'b0, esum};\n  wire              is_sub = sh_n < sh_s;\n  wire [4:0]        d      = is_sub ? sh_s[4:0] : sh_n[4:0];\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter on P2={P,0}: R[k]=P[k+d-1], S=|P[d-2:0]| ----\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {P, 1\'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? (P2 >> 1)  : P2;\n  wire [22:0] q2 = d[1] ? (q1 >> 2)  : q1;\n  wire        k2 = d[1] & (|q1[1:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q4 = d[2] ? (q2 >> 4)  : q2;\n  wire        k4 = d[2] & (|q2[3:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q8 = d[3] ? (q4 >> 8)  : q4;\n  wire        k8 = d[3] & (|q4[7:0]);\n  wire [22:0] R  = d[4] ? (q8 >> 16) : q8;\n  wire        k16 = d[4] & (|q8[15:0]);\n  wire        S  = k2 | k4 | k8 | k16;\n\n  wire [10:0] mant    = R[11:1];\n  wire        G       = R[0];\n  wire        rup     = G & (S | mant[0]);\n  wire [11:0] rounded = {1\'b0, mant} + {11\'d0, rup};\n  wire [9:0]  frac    = rounded[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire signed [7:0] E    = {3\'b000, ppos} + {2\'b00, esum} - 8\'sd50;\n  wire signed [7:0] yexp = E + 8\'sd15 + {7\'d0, rounded[11]};\n  wire ovf    = ~is_sub & (yexp > 8\'sd30);\n  wire [4:0] e_res = is_sub ? {4\'d0, rounded[10]} : (ovf ? 5\'d31 : yexp[4:0]);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf = inf_in | ovf;\n  wire [15:0] y_norm = {s_y, e_res, frac};\n  wire [15:0] y_zero = {s_y, 5\'d0,  10\'d0};\n  wire [15:0] y_inf  = {s_y, 5\'d31, 10\'d0};\n  assign y = is_nan ? 16\'h7E00 : is_inf ? y_inf : zero_in ? y_zero : y_norm;\nendmodule\n', filename='design2.sv')

The baseline's critical path goes through the 22-iteration priority chain for the leading-zero count. Let me try a `casez` (balanced mux) LZ.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- baseline structure, LZ via balanced casez priority mux.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_az = ~|e_a, e_bz = ~|e_b;\n  wire e_am = &e_a,   e_bm = &e_b;\n  wire f_az = ~|f_a,  f_bz = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = e_az & f_az;\n  wire zero_b = e_bz & f_bz;\n  wire nan_a  = e_am & ~f_az;\n  wire nan_b  = e_bm & ~f_bz;\n  wire inf_a  = e_am & f_az;\n  wire inf_b  = e_bm & f_bz;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_az, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_bz, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_az ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n    sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design3.sv')

The baseline path is a deep chain. Let me build a restructured version: single barrel shifter with integrated sticky, early exponent computation, and late carry injection.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, depth-optimised.\n// One barrel shifter (with fused sticky), one rounder, exponent computed\n// ahead of the rounding carry.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (\n  input  [15:0] a,\n  input  [15:0] b,\n  output [15:0] y\n);\n  wire        s_a = a[15], s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10], e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0],  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_az = ~|e_a, e_bz = ~|e_b, e_am = &e_a, e_bm = &e_b;\n  wire f_az = ~|f_a, f_bz = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = e_az & f_az, zero_b = e_bz & f_bz;\n  wire inf_a  = e_am & f_az, inf_b  = e_bm & f_bz;\n  wire nan_in = (e_am & ~f_az) | (e_bm & ~f_bz) | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_az, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_bz, f_b};\n  wire [21:0] P   = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // leading-one position of P (0 if P==0): 5-step binary search, fixed shifts\n  reg  [4:0]  p;\n  reg  [21:0] t;\n  always @* begin\n    if (|P[21:11]) begin t = P >> 11; p = 5'd11; end\n    else           begin t = P;       p = 5'd0;  end\n    if (|t[10:6]) begin t = t >> 5; p = p + 5'd5; end\n    if (|t[5:3])  begin t = t >> 3; p = p + 5'd3; end\n    if (t[2])     begin t = t >> 2; p = p + 5'd2; end\n    if (t[1])     p = p + 5'd1;\n  end\n\n  // ---- shift amount: d = max(p-10, 26-esum); absorbing branch = subnormal result ----\n  wire [5:0] esum = (e_az ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a}) + (e_bz ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b});\n  wire [6:0] pse  = {2'b0, p} + {1'b0, esum};\n  wire       is_sub = (pse < 7'd36);\n  wire [4:0] dn = p - 5'd10;\n  wire [4:0] ds = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] d  = is_sub ? ds : dn;\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter on P2={P,0}: R[k]=P[k+d-1], S=|P[d-2:0]| ----\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {P, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? (P2 >> 1)  : P2;\n  wire [22:0] q2 = d[1] ? (q1 >> 2)  : q1;\n  wire        k2 = d[1] & (|q1[1:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q4 = d[2] ? (q2 >> 4)  : q2;\n  wire        k4 = d[2] & (|q2[3:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q8 = d[3] ? (q4 >> 8)  : q4;\n  wire        k8 = d[3] & (|q4[7:0]);\n  wire [22:0] R  = d[4] ? (q8 >> 16) : q8;\n  wire        k16 = d[4] & (|q8[15:0]);\n  wire        S  = k2 | k4 | k8 | k16;\n\n  wire [10:0] mant = R[11:1];\n  wire        G    = R[0];\n  wire        rup  = G & (S | mant[0]);\n  wire [11:0] I    = {1'b0, mant} + {11'd0, rup};\n  wire        add  = is_sub ? Design5 passed but is slow. I realize the exponent path redundantly depends on `p`. Key insight: since `E = esum + d - 40 + add` works in *both* the normal and subnormal cases (with `d` = the unified shift), I can remove `p` from the exponent path entirely. Let me test that.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- unified-shift, exponent derived from the shift amount d.\n// E = esum + d - 40 + round_carry  holds for both the normal and the subnormal\n// grid, so the normaliser position never enters the exponent path.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (\n  input  [15:0] a,\n  input  [15:0] b,\n  output [15:0] y\n);\n  wire        s_a = a[15], s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10], e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0],  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_az = ~|e_a, e_bz = ~|e_b, e_am = &e_a, e_bm = &e_b;\n  wire f_az = ~|f_a, f_bz = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = e_az & f_az, zero_b = e_bz & f_bz;\n  wire inf_a  = e_am & f_az, inf_b  = e_bm & f_bz;\n  wire nan_in = (e_am & ~f_az) | (e_bm & ~f_bz) | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_az, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_bz, f_b};\n  wire [21:0] P   = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // leading-one position of P (0 if P==0): 5-step binary search\n  reg  [4:0]  p;\n  reg  [21:0] t;\n  always @* begin\n    if (|P[21:11]) begin t = P >> 11; p = 5'd11; end\n    else           begin t = P;       p = 5'd0;  end\n    if (|t[10:6]) begin t = t >> 5; p = p + 5'd5; end\n    if (|t[5:3])  begin t = t >> 3; p = p + 5'd3; end\n    if (t[2])     begin t = t >> 2; p = p + 5'd2; end\n    if (t[1])     p = p + 5'd1;\n  end\n\n  wire [5:0] esum = (e_az ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a}) + (e_bz ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b});\n  wire [6:0] pse  = {2'b0, p} + {1'b0, esum};\n  wire [4:0] dn   = p - 5'd10;\n  wire [5:0] ds   = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire       is_sub = (pse < 7'd36);\n  wire [4:0] d    = is_sub ? ds[4:0] : dn;\n\n  // barrel shifter on P2={P,0}: R[k]=P[k+d-1], S=|P[d-2:0]|\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {P, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? [ASSISTANT]
Interesting — the binary-search LZ was *worse* than the original loop. Let me revert design.sv to the known-best baseline and create a variant with a simplified exponent path (which is on the critical path).


  -> replace_file(filename='design.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // simplified exponent: pse = esum + ppos\n  wire [4:0] ppos = 5'd21 - lz;\n  wire [7:0] pse  = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff} + {3'd0, ppos};\n  wire [7:0] psec = pse + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire       underflow = (psec <= 8'd35);\n  wire       overflow  = (psec >  8'd65);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm    = psec - 8'd35;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm  = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum_of(ea_eff, eb_eff);\n  function [7:0] exp_sum_of;\n    input [4:0] x, yy;\n    begin exp_sum_of = {3'd0,x} + {3'd0,yy}; end\n  endfunction\n\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n    sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ?   -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits),\n// subnormal support.  Dual-path (normal + subnormal) normalisation with a\n// shared-nothing rounder in each path and a compact exponent/pack stage.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS   = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // leading-zero count (0 when prod == 0)\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  // below[k] = |prod[k:0]\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path (result >= 2^-14) : leading one forced to bit 10 ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = carry ? mant_round[10:1] : mant_round[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {3'd0, (5'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] psec    = pse + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       overflow= (psec >= 8'd66);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm  = psec - 8'd35;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path (result < 2^-14) : shift onto the subnormal grid ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n\n  // ---- pack (nan > zero > inf > normal/subnormal) ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns  = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_ns = is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (overflow & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac_ns};\nendmodule\n", filename='design7.sv')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// Leading-one position found in two levels: pick a 6-bit group with a 3-way\n// select, then a shallow priority encoder inside the group.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count: group select + 6-bit priority encode ----\n  function [2:0] lzc6;\n    input [5:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (v[5]) lzc6 = 3'd0;\n      else if (v[4]) lzc6 = 3'd1;\n      else if (v[3]) lzc6 = 3'd2;\n      else if (v[2]) lzc6 = 3'd3;\n      else if (v[1]) lzc6 = 3'd4;\n      else if (v[0]) lzc6 = 3'd5;\n      else           lzc6 = 3'd6;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire h1 = |prod[21:16];\n  wire h0 = |prod[15:10];\n  wire [5:0] body = h1 ? prod[21:16] : h0 ? prod[15:10] : prod[9:0];\n  wire [2:0] l6 = lzc6(body);\n  wire [5:0] lz = h1 ? {1'b0, l6} : h0 ? (6'd6 + {3'd0, l6}) : (6'd12 + {3'd0, l6});\n\n  // below[k] = |prod[k:0]\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 6'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = carry ? mant_round[10:1] : mant_round[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {2'd0, (6'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] psec    = pse + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       overflow= (psec >= 8'd66);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= (psec - 8'd35)[4:0];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns  = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_ns = is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (overflow & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac_ns};\nendmodule\n", filename='design8.sv')

(prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = carry ? mant_round[10:1] : mant_round[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {3'd0, (5'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] psec    = pse + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       overflow= (psec >= 8'd66);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= (psec - 8'd35)[0+:5];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? [ASSISTANT]
Key insight: when rounding overflows, `mant+rup` wraps to 0, so the fraction bits are the same whether or not the carry is taken. That removes a mux. Let me also restructure the exponent so only a small increment depends on the rounding carry.




  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// Normal + subnormal normalisation paths run in parallel; the rounding carry\n// only ever feeds a narrow 5-bit increment and a one-gate overflow flag.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant       = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire        guard  = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire        sticky = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire        rup    = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] mr     = mant + {10'd0, rup};       // wraps to 0 if it overflows\n  wire        carry  = mr[10] ^ mant[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = mr[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {3'd0, (5'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] epre    = pse - 8'd35;\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       gt30    = (epre > 8'd30);\n  wire       eq30    = (epre == 8'd30);\n  wire       overflow= gt30 | (eq30 & carry);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= (epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry}) | {5{overflow}};\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? [ASSISTANT]
Excellent — new best (1174 ps, 126030). Two more simplifications: the `epre==30 & carry` overflow case is naturally correct (mant wraps to 0 → exp becomes 31), and the rounding add only needs 10 bits.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// Normal + subnormal normalisation computed in parallel; the rounding carry\n// feeds only a narrow increment and the fraction wraps to zero on carry-out,\n// so an overflowed normal result naturally packs as the correct infinity.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [9:0]        mant       = shifted[9:0];\n\n  wire        guard  = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire        sticky = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire        rup    = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] mr     = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};   // wraps to 0 on overflow\n  wire        carry  = mr[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = mr[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {3'd0, (5'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] epre    = pse - 8'd35;          // == unbiased field value for normals\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       ovf     = (epre > 8'd30);       // epre==30 with carry is already inf\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry};\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns  = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_ns = is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (ovf & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac_ns};\nendmodule\n", filename='design11.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
The tool is deterministic, so design10 (1174 ps) is a solid best. Key insight from its path: the front-end (hidden-bit + exponent) delays the multiplier start by ~178 ps. Let me try to shorten both.




  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// Front-end optimised: the significand hidden bits are the raw exponent OR\n// reductions (one gate level), and the biased exponent is a single 3-input\n// signed sum.  Rounding overflow is absorbed naturally into the pack.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_nz  = |e_a;\n  wire eb_nz  = |e_b;\n  wire e_a_ones = &e_a;\n  wire e_b_ones = &e_b;\n  wire f_a_zero = ~|f_a;\n  wire f_b_zero = ~|f_b;\n\n  wire zero_a = ~ea_nz & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = ~eb_nz & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // significands with hidden bit; subnormal/zero get no hidden one\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {ea_nz, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {eb_nz, f_b};\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant       = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire        guard  = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire        sticky = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire        rup    = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);\n  wire [11:0] mr     = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};   // wraps to 0 on overflow\n  wire        carry  = mr[11];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = mr[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent: epre = ea_eff + eb_eff - lz - 14 (single signed sum) ----\n  wire [5:0]  esum  = (ea_nz ? {1'b0, e_a} : 6'd1) + (eb_nz ? {1'b0, e_b} : 6'd1);\n  wire signed [7:0] epre = {2'b0, esum} - {3'b0, lz} - 8'sd14;\n  wire       is_sub  = (epre <= 8'sd0);\n  wire       ovf     = (epre > 8'sd30);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry};\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - {2'b0, esum};\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns  = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_ns = is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (ovf & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac_ns};\nendmodule\n", filename='design12.sv')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// design10 structure with a shortened leading-zero count: choose one of three\n// 6-bit groups with a shallow select, then a tiny priority encoder inside.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count: 3-way group select + 6-bit priority encode ----\n  function [2:0] lzc6;\n    input [5:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (v[5]) lzc6 = 3'd0;\n      else if (v[4]) lzc6 = 3'd1;\n      else if (v[3]) lzc6 = 3'd2;\n      else if (v[2]) lzc6 = 3'd3;\n      else if (v[1]) lzc6 = 3'd4;\n      else if (v[0]) lzc6 = 3'd5;\n      else           lzc6 = 3'd6;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire h1 = |prod[21:16];\n  wire h0 = |prod[15:10];\n  wire [5:0] body = h1 ? prod[21:16] : h0 ? prod[15:10] : prod[9:0];\n  wire [2:0] l6 = lzc6(body);\n  wire [5:0] lz = h1 ? {1'b0, l6} : h0 ? (6'd6 + {3'd0, l6}) : (6'd12 + {3'd0, l6});\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- normal path ----\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 6'd11);\n  wire [3:0]        sr         = 4'd11 - lz[3:0];\n  wire [3:0]        sl         = lz[3:0] - 4'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant       = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire        guard  = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire        sticky = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire        rup    = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] mr     = mant + {10'd0, rup};       // wraps to 0 if it overflows\n  wire        carry  = mr[10] ^ mant[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = mr[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] pse     = exp_sum + {2'd0, (6'd21 - lz)};\n  wire [7:0] epre    = pse - 8'd35;\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 8'd35);\n  wire       gt30    = (epre > 8'd30);\n  wire       eq30    = (epre == 8'd30);\n  wire       overflow= gt30 | (eq30 & carry);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm= (epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry}) | {5{overflow}};\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - exp_sum;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? Important realization: `d` is provably ≤ 15 (normals: p−10 ≤ 11; subnormals: is_sub forces esum ≥ 11 so 26−esum ≤ 15). So the shift-by-16 stage is dead logic. Let me remove it.




  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n// design10 structure.  The unified shift amount is provably <= 15 (normals use\n// p-10 <= 11; a subnormal result forces exp_sum >= 11 so 26-exp_sum <= 15), so\n// the barrel shifter needs only the 1/2/4/8 stages - no 16 stage.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // leading-zero count (0 when prod == 0) -> leading-one position p = 21-lz\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n  wire [4:0] ppos = 5'd21 - lz;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- unified shift amount d = max(p-10, 26-exp_sum), both <= 15 ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_sum = (e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a) + (e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b);\n  wire [5:0] pse     = {1'b0, exp_sum} + {1'b0, ppos};\n  wire       is_sub  = (pse <= 6'd35);\n  wire [3:0] dn      = ppos[3:0] - 4'd10;\n  wire [3:0] ds      = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [3:0] d       = is_sub ? ds : dn;\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter on P2 = {P,0} : R[k] = P[k+d-1], S = |P[d-2:0]| ----\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? (P2 >> 1) : P2;\n  wire [22:0] q2 = d[1] ? (q1 >> 2) : q1;\n  wire        k2 = d[1] & (|q1[1:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q4 = d[2] ? (q2 >> 4) : q2;\n  wire        k4 = d[2] & (|q2[3:0]);\n  wire [22:0] R  = d[3] ? (q4 >> 8) : q4;\n  wire        k8 = d[3] & (|q4[7:0]);\n  wire        S  = k2 | k4 | k8;\n\n  wire [10:0] mant  = R[11:1];\n  wire        G     = R[0];\n  wire        rup   = G & (S | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] mr    = mant + {10'd0, rup};          // wraps to 0 on overflow\n  wire        carry = mr[10] ^ mant[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = mr[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] es    = {3'd0, exp_sum};\n  wire [7:0] epre  = es + {2'd0, (6'd21 - lz)} - 8'd35;   // biased field (normals)\n  wire       gt30  = (epre > 8'd30);\n  wire       eq30  = (epre == 8'd30);\n  wire       ovf   = gt30 | (eq30 & carry);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry}) | {5{ovf}};\n\n  // ---- subnormal path ----\n  wire [7:0] sub_shift_c = 8'd26 - es;\n  integer    total_shift;\n  always @* total_shift = sub_shift_c[5:0];\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted = (sub_shift_c < 8'd22) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns  = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_ns = is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (ovf & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac_ns};\nendmodule\n", filename='design15.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
Major simplification found: the normal and subnormal paths both derive from the same shifter output `R` and sticky `S`. The round-up condition and the fraction bits coincide, so just **one 11-bit adder** is needed. Let me implement this.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier, subnormal support.\n//\n// P = m_a*m_b (22b).  Let d = max(p-10, 26-exp_sum) with p the leading-one\n// position; both branches are provably <= 15, so R = {P,0} >> d needs only the\n// 1/2/4/8 barrel stages.  R[k] = P[k+d-1], so\n//   normal : significand = R[11:1], guard = R[0]\n//   sub    : significand = R[10:1], guard = R[0]\n// both with sticky = OR P[d-2:0] = the shifter's own shifted-out OR (S).  The\n// round-up decision is identical in both cases and the fraction is the low 10\n// bits of R[11:1]+rup, so a single 11-bit adder serves both; its bit 10 is the\n// subnormal carry and bit 11 the normal carry.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam MAX_E  = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire f_a_zero = (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire f_b_zero = (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & f_a_zero;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & f_b_zero;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & ~f_a_zero;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & ~f_b_zero;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & f_a_zero;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & f_b_zero;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = (e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a}) + (e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b});\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // leading-zero count (0 when prod == 0); p = 21-lz\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n  wire [4:0] ppos = 5'd21 - lz;\n\n  // ---- unified shift amount ----\n  wire [6:0] pse    = {1'b0, exp_sum} + {2'b0, ppos};\n  wire       is_sub = (pse <= 7'd35);\n  wire [3:0] dn     = ppos[3:0] - 4'd10;\n  wire [3:0] ds     = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [3:0] d      = is_sub ? ds : dn;\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter: R = {P,0} >> d, S = OR of shifted-out bits ----\n  wire [22:0] P2 = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q1 = d[0] ? (P2 >> 1) : P2;\n  wire [22:0] q2 = d[1] ? (q1 >> 2) : q1;\n  wire        k2 = d[1] & (|q1[1:0]);\n  wire [22:0] q4 = d[2] ? (q2 >> 4) : q2;\n  wire        k4 = d[2] & (|q2[3:0]);\n  wire [22:0] R  = d[3] ? (q4 >> 8) : q4;\n  wire        k8 = d[3] & (|q4[7:0]);\n  wire        S  = k2 | k4 | k8;\n\n  // ---- single rounding adder for both ranges ----\n  wire [10:0] X   = R[11:1];\n  wire        rup = R[0] & (S | R[1]);\n  wire [11:0] XR  = {1'b0, X} + {11'd0, rup};\n  wire [9:0]  frac = XR[9:0];\n  wire        carry   = XR[11];   // normal significand overflow\n  wire        carry_s = XR[10];   // subnormal roll-over to the smallest normal\n\n  // ---- exponent ----\n  wire [7:0] es   = {2'd0, exp_sum};\n  wire [7:0] epre = es + {2'd0, ppos} - 8'd35;    // biased exponent (normals)\n  wire       gt30 = (epre > 8'd30);\n  wire       eq30 = (epre == 8'd30);\n  wire       ovf  = gt30 | (eq30 & carry);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (epre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry}) | {5{ovf}};\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_ns = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00\n           : zero_in ? {s_y, 5'd0, 10'd0}\n           : (inf_in | (ovf & ~is_sub)) ? {s_y, 5'd31, 10'd0}\n           : {s_y, exp_ns, frac};\nendmodule\n", filename='design16.sv')
